`train_decoder.py`\n    22→\n    23→---\n    24→\n    25→## Step 1: Reference Code Exploration\n    26→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    27→\n    28→### Key Functions Identified\n    29→| Function | File | Stage | Purpose |\n    30→|----------|------|-------|---------|\n    31→| `loadObjs` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/loadObjs.m` | LOADING | Loads each session `obj` `.mat` file and normalizes missing struct fields (`meta`, `ex`, `me`). |\n    32→| `findDataFn` | `code/utils/findDataFn.m` | LOADING | Resolves the `data_structure_*` filename for a given animal/date. |\n    33→| `loadJEB*_ALMVideo`, `loadJGR*_ALMVideo`, etc. | `code/DataLoadingScripts/Recording and video/*.m` | LOADING | Enumerate included ephys+video sessions and assign the relevant probe number(s). |\n    34→| `loadSessionData` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/loadSessionData.m` | LOADING | Main reference entry point: loops sessions/probes, calls `processData`, then concatenates across probes into session-level outputs. |\n    35→| `processData` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/processData.m` | PROCESSING | Performs trial selection, cluster selection, spike alignment, PSTH/trial matrix creation, low-FR curation, and baseline statistics. |\n    36→| `findTrials` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/findTrials.m` | CURATION | Evaluates logical expressions on `obj.bp` fields to produce per-condition trial indices. |\n    37→| `findClusters` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/findClusters.m` | CURATION | Selects clusters by quality label; `'all'` excludes `garbage`, `gabrga`, `noisy`, and `real?`. |\n    38→| `alignSpikes` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/alignSpikes.m` | PROCESSING | Aligns per-spike trial times to the chosen event (`goCue`, `moveOnset`, `firstLick`, `lastLick`, `jawOnset`). |\n    39→| `getSeq` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/getSeq.m` | PROCESSING | Bins aligned spikes from `tmin:dt:tmax`, smooths them, and creates `obj.psth{probe}` and `obj.trialdat{probe}`. |\n    40→| `removeLowFRClusters` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/removeLowFRClusters.m` | CURATION | Removes neurons with mean firing rate below `lowFR` using the trial-averaged PSTH. |\n    41→| `baselineFR` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/baselineFR.m` | PROCESSING | Computes baseline firing rate and sigma from `-0.5 s` to `sample` onset. |\n    42→| `findVideoOffset` | `code/funcs/findVideoOffset.m` | PROCESSING | Computes offset between neural and video file starts for frame-time alignment. |\n    43→| `loadMotionEnergy` | `code/DataLoadingScripts/loadMotionEnergy.m` | PROCESSING | Loads session motion energy, interpolates it onto the neural time axis aligned to `params.alignEvent`, fills edge NaNs, and thresholds into move/non-move. |\n    44→| `getKinematicsFromVideo` | `code/funcs/kinematics/getKinematicsFromVideo.m` | PROCESSING | Interpolates DLC coordinates to the aligned trial time axis and derives x/y displacement and velocity per feature. |\n    45→| `findVelocity` | `code/funcs/kinematics/findVelocity.m` | PROCESSING | Computes per-trial velocity from interpolated trajectories; non-tongue features are baseline-subtracted, tongue NaNs become zero velocity. |\n    46→| `getKinematics` | `code/funcs/kinematics/getKinematics.m` | PROCESSING | Builds the full behavioral feature tensor by combining DLC kinematics, derived tongue angle/length, and motion energy. |\n    47→| `loadBehavSessionData` | `code/funcs/fig3/loadBehavSessionData.m` | LOADING | Behavior-only/session helper that finds trial IDs and video offset; includes `firstLick` alignment logic. |\n    48→\n    49→### Notes\n    50→- The reference data object is a MATLAB struct `obj` with trial/task data in `obj.bp`, spike data in `obj.clu`, DLC trajectories in `obj.traj`, and sometimes motion energy in `obj.me`.\n    51→- The main ephys processing path is `loadSessionData -> processData -> findTrials/findClusters -> alignSpikes -> getSeq -> removeLowFRClusters -> baselineFR`.\n    52→- Reference analyses commonly use `params.alignEvent = 'goCue'`, which matches the decoder task requirement.\n    53→- Spike data are not converted to dF/F; these are electrophysiology sessions. Neural activity is processed as binned/smoothed firing rates.\n    54→- Neural binning is configurable. Core loaders support arbitrary `dt`; multiple figure scripts use `dt = 1/100` (10 ms) and some use `dt = 1/200` (5 ms). Final choice must be reconciled with paper/methods and data in later steps.\n    55→- `getSeq` bins spikes on `[tmin, tmax)` using `histc`, then smooths counts converted to Hz with `mySmooth`. Session-level single-trial neural data end up as `(time, neurons, trials)`.\n    56→- Cluster curation has two layers:\n    57→  - Quality filter by label using `findClusters`.\n    58→  - Firing-rate filter using `removeLowFRClusters`, typically `lowFR = 1` Hz in several figure scripts and tutorial code.\n    59→- Video/behavior streams are interpolated to the neural/session time axis rather than used at native frame rate.\n    60→- Motion energy alignment is explicit: `interp1(frameTimes - video_offset - align_event_time, me.data{trial}, obj.time + advance_movement)`, then edge NaNs are filled with nearest values.\n    61→- DLC trajectories also use frame times corrected by a 0.5 s or computed video offset depending on loader/helper path. This is important for later consistency checks on temporal alignment.\n    62→- Trial selection is driven by expressions over `obj.bp` fields such as `R`, `L`, `hit`, `miss`, `no`, `autowater`, `stim.enable`, and `early`.\n    63→- `autowater` is the code’s proxy for behavioral context: delayed response / 2AFC (`autowater == 0`) versus water-cued (`autowater == 1`).\n    64→- Choice/context decoding scripts in `code/ChoiceContextDecoding` balance trials across classes, typically use hit/miss subsets, and operate on `obj.trialdat` or interpolated kinematic tensors aligned to go cue.\n    65→- Metadata loaders encode the included sessions and which probe corresponds to ALM. Some sessions have one relevant probe and some analyses can concatenate two probes within a session.\n    66→\n    67→---\n    68→\n    69→## Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n    70→**Status**: COMPLETE\n    71→\n    72→### Data Structure\n    73→- Top-level raw-data folders:\n    74→  - `data/Ephys_Behavior`: delayed-response / water-cued sessions with neural data and separate `motionEnergy_*.mat` files.\n    75→  - `data/RandomizedDelay_Ephys_Behavior`: randomized-delay sessions; most have neural data plus separate `motionEnergy_*.mat` files.\n    76→  - `data/DelayInhibition_BilatMC_Behavior`: behavior-only inhibition sessions; no neural `clu` field, motion energy embedded inside `obj.me`.\n    77→  - `data/GoCueInhibition_BilatMC_Behavior`: behavior-only inhibition sessions; no neural `clu` field.\n    78→- File format:\n    79→  - All raw session files are MATLAB `.mat`.\n    80→  - Most `data_structure_*.mat` files are MATLAB v7.3/HDF5; at least the older `EKH1` and `EKH3` sessions are non-HDF5 MATLAB files.\n    81→  - `motionEnergy_*.mat` files load directly with SciPy and contain `me.data` and `me.moveThresh`.\n    82→- Raw session object layout:\n    83→  - Ephys sessions: `obj.bp`, `obj.clu`, `obj.ex`, `obj.meta`, `obj.pth`, `obj.sglx`, `obj.traj`, `obj.trials`.\n    84→  - Behavior-only sessions: `obj.bp`, `obj.ex`, `obj.me`, `obj.pth`, `obj.sglx`, `obj.traj`, `obj.trials`.\n    85→- Important raw variables observed:\n    86→  - `obj.bp`: trial labels (`L`, `R`, `hit`, `miss`, `no`, `autowater`, `early`, `protocol`, `stim`).\n    87→  - `obj.bp.ev`: event times including `bitStart`, `sample`, `delay`, `goCue`, `reward`, `lickL`, `lickR`.\n    88→  - `obj.clu`: per-probe spike data with fields `quality`, `site`, `spkWavs`, `tm`, `trial`, `trialtm`.\n    89→  - `obj.traj`: two camera views; each view stores per-trial lists of `frameTimes`, `ts`, `featNames`, etc.\n    90→  - External motion-energy files: `me.data` is one trace per trial; `moveThresh` is a per-session threshold.\n    91→- Representative dimensions from raw files:\n    92→  - DLC trajectory arrays are `(n_frames, 3, n_features)` per trial.\n    93→  - Example side-camera trial: `(2639, 3, 7)`; example bottom-camera trial: `(2639, 3, 10)`.\n    94→  - Example video sampling interval is about `2.52 ms` (`~396.8 Hz`).\n    95→  - Example motion-energy trial length is `2639` samples for an ephys session, consistent with the video frame count.\n    96→- Raw-data caveats found directly from the files:\n    97→  - `data_structure_JEB24_2023-10-03.mat` and `data_structure_JEB24_2023-10-04.mat` live in `RandomizedDelay_Ephys_Behavior` but have no `clu` field, so they are behavior-only for decoder purposes.\n    98→  - Those same two JEB24 sessions are also missing external `motionEnergy_*.mat` companions.\n    99→  - Probe location metadata are inconsistently populated in the raw files; some sessions record `L ALM` / `R ALM`, many store no location string.\n   100→\n   101→### Dataset Size (from data files)\n   102→| Statistic | Value |\n   103→|-----------|-------|\n   104→| Neurons (total) | 14,297 raw clusters across sessions with neural data |\n   105→| Neurons / session | mean 317.7, median 70, min 17, max 1258 (raw, pre-curation) |\n   106→| Subjects | 14 mice with neural data; 18 mice total across all raw folders |\n   107→| Sessions / subject | mean 3.21 neural sessions per mouse (range 1-10) |\n   108→| Trials (total) | 15,324 trials in sessions with neural data; 37,073 trials across all raw folders |\n   109→| Trials / session | mean 340.5, median 343, min 230, max 517 for neural-data sessions |\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n   114→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   115→\n   116→### Expected Statistics (from paper/methods)\n   117→| Statistic | Value | Source Quote |\n   118→|-----------|-------|--------------|\n   119→| Neurons (total) | Two-context task: 522 units; DR-only fixed-delay task: 1,651 units; randomized-delay task: 845 units | “two-context paradigm: 12 sessions, six mice, 522 units”; “25 sessions, nine mice, 1,651 units”; “19 sessions… 845 units” |\n   120→| Neurons / session | Two-context mean about 43.5 units/session; DR-only mean about 66.0; randomized-delay mean about 44.5 | Computed from paper totals above |\n   121→| Subjects | Two-context: 6 mice; fixed-delay DR total: 9 mice; randomized-delay: 4 mice | “12 sessions, six mice”; “25 sessions, nine mice”; “19 sessions… four mice” |\n   122→| Sessions / subject | Two-context mean 2.0; fixed-delay DR mean about 2.8; randomized-delay mean about 4.8 | Computed from paper totals above |\n   123→| Trials (total) | Not explicitly tabulated in text | Not numerically stated in methods/paper text provided |\n   124→| Trials / session | Not explicitly tabulated in text; block structure is described | “approximately 100 DR trials” at session start, then “10–25 trials” per interleaved block |\n   125→| Neural data time bin | 5 ms in published decoding analyses | “each bin is 5 ms” |\n   126→| Behavior data time bin | 400 Hz raw video, approximately 2.5 ms per frame; decoding analyses also operate on 5 ms bins | “High-speed video was captured (400-Hz frame rate)” and “each bin is 5 ms” |\n   127→| Reward rate | Reward delivered for correct DR choices; WC reward is approximately 3 µl at a random port | “water reward was delivered” / “an approximately 3-µl water reward was presented randomly” |\n   128→| Task/behavior statistic 1 | Fixed-delay DR performance criterion: >70% accuracy before/for expert animals | “reached at least 70% accuracy”; “became experts (>70% accuracy …)” |\n   129→| Task/behavior statistic 2 | Randomized-delay early-lick criterion: <20% | “became experts (>70% accuracy and <20% early lick rate)” |\n   130→| Task/behavior statistic 3 | Behavioral analysis sessions required at least 40 correct DR trials per direction and 20 correct WC trials per direction | “at least 40 correct DR trials for each direction… and 20 correct WC trials for each direction” |\n   131→\n   132→\n   133→### Processing Details\n   134→- Task structure:\n   135→  - DR task: 1.3 s auditory sample, then delay, then 10 ms auditory go cue.\n   136→  - Standard fixed-delay DR task used 0.9 s delay for 12 mice; one mouse had 0.7 s delay that was linearly time-warped to 0.9 s in analyses.\n   137→  - WC task omits auditory cues; a water drop appears at a random side/time.\n   138→  - Two-context sessions begin with about 100 DR trials, then alternate WC/DR blocks of 10-25 trials; sessions begin in DR.\n   139→  - Randomized-delay task uses six delay values: 0.3, 0.6, 1.2, 1.8, 2.4, 3.6 s.\n   140→- Temporal alignment:\n   141→  - Methods and figure text repeatedly describe activity, movement, and decoding relative to the go cue (or water drop in WC plots); the user task specifically requires go-cue alignment, matching the main neural alignment used in the code and paper.\n   142→  - ITI context analyses use the 300 ms before sample onset.\n   143→- Kinematic/video processing:\n   144→  - Two 400 Hz cameras.\n   145→  - Tongue, jaw, nose tracked in both views; paws tracked from bottom view only.\n   146→  - Use x/y position and velocity per tracked feature.\n   147→  - Missing values are filled with nearest values for all features except the tongue.\n   148→  - Tongue angle and length are derived from the bottom camera.\n   149→- Motion energy processing:\n   150→  - Frame-wise motion energy is based on the absolute difference between the median of the next five frames and previous five frames.\n   151→  - Each frame is summarized by the 99th percentile across pixels.\n   152→  - Move/non-move threshold is set manually per session from bimodal distributions.\n   153→- Decoding in the paper:\n   154→  - Equal numbers of correct left and right lick trials were used for choice decoding.\n   155→  - Separate models were trained at each time bin for neural and kinematic decoding.\n   156→  - Models used ridge regularization and four-fold cross-validation.\n   157→  - Chance accuracy was computed by shuffling labels.\n   158→- Neural recording/processing:\n   159→  - ALM extracellular recordings from H2 and Neuropixels 1.0 probes.\n   160→  - Spike sorting via JRCLUST and/or Kilosort 3 with manual Phy curation.\n   161→  - Sessions were included only if they had at least 10 units.\n   162→  - For most analyses, all units with firing rate >1 Hz were included.\n   163→  - For subspace alignment and some single-unit analyses, only well-isolated single units with firing rate >1 Hz were included.\n   164→\n   165→### Curation Steps\n   166→\n   167→**Neuron curation rules**:\n   168→- Manual spike-sorting curation distinguishes well-isolated single units from multiunits.\n   169→- Recording sessions must have at least 10 units to be analyzed.\n   170→- A >1 Hz firing-rate threshold is applied.\n   171→- Some analyses use only well-isolated single units >1 Hz; other analyses include all units >1 Hz.\n   172→\n   173→**Trial curation rules**:\n   174→- Early-lick trials are omitted from analyses.\n   175→- Ignore / no-response trials are omitted from behavioral analyses.\n   176→- Behavioral analyses require enough correct trials per class: at least 40 correct DR trials per direction and 20 correct WC trials per direction.\n   177→- Choice decoding uses equal numbers of correct left and right lick trials.\n   178→\n   179→### Decoders Trained\n   180→| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n   181→| Choice from uninstructed movement / neural activity | Reported qualitatively as above chance over time; exact numeric curve values are shown graphically, not tabulated in text |\n   182→| Context from kinematics / neural activity | Reported qualitatively as above chance over time, including during ITI; exact numeric curve values are shown graphically, not tabulated in text |\n   183→\n   184→---\n   185→\n   186→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   187→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   188→\n   189→### Discrepancies Found\n   190→| Topic | Code says | Data shows | Paper says | Resolution |\n   191→|-------|-----------|------------|------------|------------|\n   192→| Randomized-delay session count | `Figure3i.m` loads JEB11 (2), JEB12 (2), JEB23 (7; `2023-10-20` commented out), JEB24 (8) = 19 sessions | Raw folder has 22 files; 2 (`JEB24_2023-10-03`, `JEB24_2023-10-04`) have no `clu`, leaving 20 neural-capable files | “19 sessions… four mice” | Resolved: the analyzed randomized-delay set is the 19 sessions explicitly selected by the loader files, not every raw file in the folder. |\n   193→| Fixed-delay DR session/mouse count | `Figure3d.m` / `Figure3f.m` load 25 sessions spanning 10 subject IDs (`JEB6`, `JEB7`, `EKH1`, `EKH3`, `JGR2`, `JGR3`, `JEB13`, `JEB14`, `JEB15`, `JEB19`) | Raw `Ephys_Behavior` folder contains exactly those 25 sessions from 10 mice | “25 sessions, nine mice” | Partially resolved: session count is consistent at 25, but mouse count differs by one between paper text and released code/data. Treat this as a likely text/reporting discrepancy; follow the explicit session lists in code. |\n   194→| Two-context dataset size | `Figure8a_thru_c.m` / `Figure8d.m` load 12 sessions from 7 subject IDs (`JEB6`, `JEB7`, `EKH1`, `EKH3`, `JGR2`, `JGR3`, `JEB19`) | Raw fixed-delay folder contains 22 sessions with both `autowater` states present | “12 sessions, six mice, 522 units” | Resolved for session count but not mouse count: use the 12-session subset defined in the code for context analyses. The six-vs-seven mouse discrepancy is left as a text/code inconsistency. |\n   195→| Neuron totals | Core loaders start from all clusters, then apply quality and low-FR filters; specific analyses may further restrict to well-isolated single units >1 Hz | Raw neural files contain 14,297 clusters across 45 neural sessions | Paper reports 522 units (two-context), 1,651 units (fixed-delay DR total), 845 units (randomized delay) | Resolved: raw release contains unfiltered clusters and extra sessions/files; paper counts are post-curation analysis subsets, not raw file totals. |\n   196→| Region labels | Loader files explicitly pick the ALM probe for most sessions, and `loadSessionData` concatenates both probes when `meta.probe = [1 2]` | Raw `ex.probe.loc` strings are often missing, but can include non-ALM labels on pooled sessions (for example `JEB15_2022-07-26` has `L ALM` and `L M1TJ`) | Paper emphasizes ALM recordings, but the released session metadata can include additional probe locations in pooled sessions | Resolved: preserve per-neuron region labels from raw metadata for the reference-selected probe(s), rather than forcing every neuron to `ALM`. |\n   197→| Time bin size | Code examples use both 10 ms (`dt=1/100`) and 5 ms (`dt=1/200`) depending on figure/script | Raw video is about 400 Hz (`~2.5 ms`) and spikes are stored as event times | Paper’s decoding methods explicitly state 5 ms bins | Resolved: for decoder-matched conversion, prioritize 5 ms bins over generic 10 ms examples. |\n   198→| Motion-energy availability | Code expects external `motionEnergy_*.mat` for ephys and embedded `obj.me` for behavior-only sessions | Two randomized-delay files (`JEB24_2023-10-03`, `JEB24_2023-10-04`) lack both `clu` and external motion energy | Paper describes motion energy as available for movement analyses | Resolved: exclude sessions without neural data from the decoder dataset; missing motion energy on those two sessions is not relevant once excluded. |\n   199→\n   200→---\n   201→\n   202→## Step 5: Mapping Planning\n   203→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   204→\n   205→### Variable Mapping\n   206→| Source Variable | Target Field | Transform | Reference Code Function(s) | Notes |\n   207→|-----------------|--------------|-----------|----------------------------|-------|\n   208→| `obj.clu{probe}.trialtm`, `obj.clu{probe}.trial`, `obj.bp.ev.goCue` | `neural` | Align each spike to go cue, bin at 5 ms from `-2.5` to `+2.5` s, smooth with the reference kernel, convert to firing rate, arrange as `(neurons, time)` per trial | `alignSpikes`, `getSeq`, `removeLowFRClusters` | Use the probe(s) selected by the reference loader files; concatenate probes within a session if code does so. |\n   209→| Common aligned time axis | `input[0]` | Continuous time-from-go-cue series, same for every trial, shape `(1, time)` | `getSeq` / paper decoding methods | Input name: `time_from_go_cue`. |\n   210→| `obj.bp.R` / `obj.bp.L` | `output[0]` | Per-trial categorical label: left=`0`, right=`1` | `findTrials`, task definitions in methods | Exclude early and no-response trials so choice/outcome are well-defined. |\n   211→| `obj.bp.autowater` | `output[1]` | Per-trial categorical label remapped to WC=`0`, DR=`1` | `findTrials`; paper/task description | Raw code uses `autowater==1` as WC. |\n   212→| `obj.bp.hit` / `obj.bp.miss` | `output[2]` | Per-trial categorical label: miss=`0`, hit=`1` | `getOutcome` | Ignore/no-response trials excluded rather than forced into incorrect. |\n   213→| DLC trajectories for tongue-related features | `output[3]` | Reference kinematic interpolation and velocity computation, then aggregate to a single tongue-speed trace and bin by per-session median | `getKinematicsFromVideo`, `findVelocity`, `getKinematics` | Because the decoder spec needs one tongue-velocity output, aggregate across tongue velocity channels after reference processing. |\n   214→| DLC trajectories for paw-related features | `output[4]` | Reference kinematic interpolation and velocity computation, then aggregate to a single paw-speed trace and bin by per-session median | `getKinematicsFromVideo`, `findVelocity`, `getKinematics` | Paws are tracked in the bottom view only. |\n   215→| Motion energy traces (`motionEnergy_*.mat` or embedded `obj.me`) | `output[5]` | Align/interpolate to neural time axis using reference logic, then bin by per-session median | `loadMotionEnergy`, `loadMotionEnergy_Behav` | The paper’s manual move threshold is not used because the decoder task explicitly asks for 50th-percentile binning. |\n   216→\n   217→### Key Decisions\n   218→1. **Session inclusion**: Include only neural sessions represented in the reference ephys loaders.\n   219→   Fixed-delay set: all 25 sessions in `Ephys_Behavior`.\n   220→   Randomized-delay set: the 19-session subset encoded by `loadJEB11_ALMVideo`, `loadJEB12_ALMVideo`, `loadJEB23_ALMVideo`, and `loadJEB24_ALMVideo`.\n   221→   Behavior-only inhibition sessions are excluded because they have no neural activity.\n   222→2. **Trial inclusion**: Use only control/non-stimulation trials with valid behavioral labels.\n   223→   Exclude `stim.enable`, `early`, and `no` trials to match the reference analyses and keep output definitions unambiguous.\n   224→3. **Neural curation**: Apply the released-code quality filter (`all` except garbage/noisy-like labels), then remove units with firing rate `<= 1 Hz`, and exclude sessions with fewer than 10 remaining units.\n   225→4. **Temporal representation**: Use go-cue alignment and a common 5 ms bin width over `[-2.5 s, +2.5 s)`.\n   226→   This follows the paper’s explicit decoding-bin specification while preserving the baseline window used in the methods and the code.\n   227→5. **Context encoding**: Represent behavioral context as WC=`0`, DR=`1`, even though raw `autowater` uses the opposite polarity.\n   228→6. **Outcome encoding**: Represent only miss versus hit.\n   229→   Ignore trials are removed instead of being merged into incorrect.\n   230→7. **Kinematic scalar outputs**: After reproducing the reference video interpolation and per-feature velocity calculation, collapse the multi-feature representation to scalar tongue-speed and paw-speed traces using aggregate speed across the relevant tracked points.\n   231→   This is a required deviation from the reference feature set because the decoder task asks for exactly one tongue-velocity output and one paw-velocity output.\n   232→8. **Motion-energy binning**: Use the reference aligned continuous trace, but discretize with the task-mandated per-session median instead of the paper’s manually chosen move threshold.\n   233→9. **Brain-region labels**: Store per-neuron labels from the raw selected probe metadata, preserving hemisphere-specific ALM labels and any additional locations present in pooled sessions; fall back to `ALM` only when the raw location string is missing.\n   234→\n   235→### Planned Sanity Checks\n   236→- [ ] Neural spot-check: for selected session/trial/neuron, verify converted binned firing rates match raw spike times aligned to go cue and smoothed with the same kernel.\n   237→- [ ] Input check: verify `input[0]` exactly matches the converted time axis for every session/trial.\n   238→- [ ] Trial-label check: compare converted lick direction, context, and outcome against raw `bp` fields for at least three manually chosen trials in multiple sessions.\n   239→- [ ] Motion-energy alignment check: interpolate raw motion-energy traces directly from the source files for selected trials and verify equality with converted continuous traces before discretization.\n   240→- [ ] Kinematic check: recompute tongue/paw continuous speed from raw DLC trajectories for selected trials/timepoints and verify agreement with the conversion code before median-threshold binning.\n   241→- [ ] Session-count check: verify converted sessions equal the reference loader-defined neural session lists (25 fixed-delay + 19 randomized-delay = 44 sessions).\n   242→\n   243→---\n   244→\n   245→## Step 6: Script Development\n   246→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   247→\n   248→[Implementation notes]\n   249→- Implemented `convert_data.py` as a CLI script with:\n   250→  - `python -u convert_data.py <outpicklefile>`\n   251→  - `--full`\n   252→  - `--sample`\n   253→  - `--show-processing`\n   254→- The script:\n   255→  - Parses the reference MATLAB loader files to recover the analyzed session lists and selected probes.\n   256→  - Handles both single-probe entries and pooled dual-probe entries like `meta.probe = [1 2]`, matching the concatenation logic in `loadSessionData.m`.\n   257→  - Uses `pymatreader` to load both v7.3/HDF5 and older MATLAB files into a consistent Python representation.\n   258→  - Reproduces the reference neural processing steps needed here: go-cue alignment, 5 ms binning, causal Gaussian smoothing, code-matched quality filtering, and a 1 Hz low-FR filter.\n   259→  - Reproduces reference video/motion processing: video offset correction, interpolation to the neural time axis, per-feature velocity calculation, and motion-energy alignment.\n   260→  - Assigns `brain_region_idx` per neuron from raw `ex.probe.loc` metadata instead of using a fixed hard-coded region list.\n   261→  - Builds the target dictionary and validates it with `verify_data_format` before saving.\n   262→- Smoke test completed successfully:\n   263→  - Sample mode processed `JEB6_2021-04-18` and `JEB11_2022-05-10`.\n   264→  - Output: 525 trials total, 91 neurons total.\n   265→\n   266→Code inefficiencies identified:\n   267→- Session loading from large MATLAB files is the main cost.\n   268→- Kinematic interpolation and per-neuron spike binning dominate runtime.\n   269→\n   270→Code speedups added:\n   271→- Avoided full-session alpha: float = 2.5) -> np.ndarray:\n   225→    if n <= 1:\n   226→        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)\n   227→    m = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - (n - 1) / 2.0\n   228→    sigma = (n - 1) / (2.0 * alpha)\n   229→    return np.exp(-0.5 * (m / sigma) ** 2)\n   230→\n   231→\n   232→def causal_gaussian_smooth(x: np.ndarray, n: int, bctype: str = BCTYPE) -> np.ndarray:\n   233→    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)\n   234→    if n <= 1:\n   235→        return x.astype(np.float32, copy=True)\n   236→\n   237→    squeeze = False\n   238→    if x.ndim == 1:\n   239→        x = x[:, \"zeropad\":\n   246→        x_filt = np.concatenate([np.zeros((n, x.shape[1]), dtype=x.dtype), x], axis=0)\n   247→        trim = n\n   248→    else:\n   249→        x_filt = x\n   250→        trim = 0\n   251→\n   252→    kern = gaussian_window(n)\n   253→    kern[: n // 2] = 0.0\n   254→    kern /= kern.sum()\n   255→\n   256→    out = np.empty_like(x_filt, dtype=np.float64)\n   257→    for col 0].astype(np.float32)\n   263→    return out.astype(np.float32)\n   264→\n   265→\n   266→def fill_nearest_1d(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n   267→    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32).copy()\n   268→    mask = np.isnan(x)\n   269→    if not mask.any():\n   270→        return x\n   271→    valid = np.flatnonzero(~mask)\n   272→    if valid.size == 0:\n   273→        return x\n   274→    idx = np.arange(x.size)\n   275→    pos = np.searchsorted(valid, idx)\n   276→    left = valid[np.clip(pos - 1, 0, valid.size - 1)]\n   277→    right = valid[np.clip(pos, 0, valid.size - 1)]\n   278→    choose_left = np.abs(idx - left) <= np.abs(right - idx)\n   279→    nearest = np.where(choose_left, left, right)\n   280→    x[mask] = x[nearest[mask]]\n   281→    return x\n   282→\n   283→\n str) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n   416→    xvel = np.full_like(xpos, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   417→    yvel = np.full_like(ypos, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   418→    for trial in n_trials == 0:\n   600→        return 0.0\n   601→    return n_spikes / (n_trials * (TMAX - TMIN))\n   602→\n   603→\n   604→def binned_neuron_trials(\n   605→    probe: dict,\n   606→    neuron_index: int,\n   607→    align_times: np.ndarray,\n   608→    keep_trials_0based: np.ndarray,\n   609→    edges: np.ndarray,\n   610→) -> np.ndarray:\n   611→    trials = to_vector(probe[\"trial\"][neuron_index], int)\n   612→    trialtm = to_vector(probe[\"trialtm\"][neuron_index], float)\n   613→    trial_to_keep = np.full(align_times.size + 1, -1, dtype=np.int32)\n   614→    trial_to_keep[keep_trials_0based + 1] = np.arange(keep_trials_0based.size, dtype=np.int32)\n   615→\n   616→    keep_index = trial_to_keep[trials]\n   617→    mask = keep_index >= 0\n   618→    keep_index = keep_index[mask]\n   619→    aligned = trialtm[mask] - align_times[trials[mask] - 1]\n   620→    bin_index = np.floor((aligned - TMIN) / DT).astype(np.int64)\n   621→    valid = (bin_index >= 0) & (bin_index < edges.size - 1)\n   622→\n   623→    counts = np.zeros((edges.size - 1, keep_trials_0based.size), dtype=np.float32)\n   624→    np.add.at(counts, (bin_index[valid], keep_index[valid]), 1.0)\n   625→    rates = counts / DT\n   626→    return causal_gaussian_smooth(rates, SMOOTH, BCTYPE)\n   627→\n   628→\n   629→def select_neurons(\n   630→    obj: dict,\n   631→    spec: SessionSpec,\n   632→    trial_mask: np.ndarray,\n   633→) -> Tuple[List[SelectedNeuron], List[dict]]:\n   634→    probes = normalize_probe_container(obj.get(\"clu\"))\n   635→    align_times = to_vector(obj[\"bp\"][\"ev\"][\"goCue\"], float)\n   636→    selected: List[SelectedNeuron] = []\n   637→    probe_summaries: List[dict] = []\n   638→\n   639→    for probe_num in spec.probe:\n   640→        probe_zero = probe_num - 1\n   641→        if probes is None or probe_zero >= len(probes):\n   642→            raise ValueError(f\"{spec.session_id}: selected probe {probe_num} missing from raw file\")\n   643→        probe = probes[probe_zero]\n   644→        if probe is None:\n   645→            raise ValueError(f\"{spec.session_id}: selected probe {probe_num} has no cluster data\")\n   646→\n   647→        qualities = [normalize_string(q) for q in probe[\"quality\"]]\n   648→        quality_mask = quality_keep_mask(qualities)\n   649→        loc = get_probe_loc(obj, probe_zero)\n   650→        region_label = session_brain_region_label(loc)\n   651→        kept_indices = []\n   652→        for neuron_index in np.flatnonzero(quality_mask):\n   653→            mean_fr = neuron_mean_fr(probe, int(neuron_index), align_times, trial_mask)\n   654→            if mean_fr > LOW_FR:\n   655→                kept_indices.append(int(neuron_index))\n   656→                selected.append(\n   657→                    SelectedNeuron(\n   658→                        probe_num=probe_num,\n   659→                        neuron_index=int(neuron_index),\n   660→                        region_label=region_label,\n   661→                    )\n   662→                )\n   663→\n   664→        probe_summaries.append(\n   665→            {\n   666→                \"probe_num\": probe_num,\n   667→                \"probe_loc\": loc,\n   668→                \"region_label\": region_label,\n   669→                \"n_neurons_raw_quality_filtered\": int(np.sum(quality_mask)),\n   670→                \"n_neurons_kept\": int(len(kept_indices)),\n   671→            }\n   672→        )\n   673→\n   674→    return selected, probe_summaries\n   675→\n   676→\n   677→def max_supported_trial(selected_neurons: Sequence[SelectedNeuron], probes) -> int:\n   678→    max_trial = 0\n   679→    for selected in selected_neurons:\n   680→        probe = probes[selected.probe_num - 1]\n   681→        trials = to_vector(probe[\"trial\"][selected.neuron_index], int)\n   682→        if trials.size:\n   683→            max_trial = max(max_trial, int(np.max(trials)))\n   684→    return max_trial\n   685→\n   686→\n   687→def session_brain_region_label(loc: str) -> str:\n   688→    loc = normalize_string(loc)\n   689→    return loc if loc else \"ALM\"\n   690→\n   691→\n   692→def plot_processing(\n   693→    spec: SessionSpec,\n   694→    session_info: dict,\n   695→    neural_trials: List[np.ndarray],\n   696→    time_centers:   794→\n   795→    fig.tight_layout()\n   796→    fig.savefig(ROOT / f\"processing_{spec.session_id}.png\", dpi=150)\n   797→    plt.close(fig)\n   798→\n   799→\n   800→def process_session(\n   801→    spec: SessionSpec,\n   802→    show_processing: bool,\n   803→) -> Optional[Tuple[dict, List[np.ndarray], List[np.ndarray], List[np.ndarray], np.ndarray, int, np.ndarray]]:\n   804→    t0 = time.perf_counter()\n   805→    obj = read_mat(spec.data_path)[\"obj\"]\n   806→    probes = normalize_probe_container(obj.get(\"clu\"))\n   807→    align_times = to_vector(obj[\"bp\"][\"ev\"][\"goCue\"], float)\n   808→    time_edges = make_time_edges()\n   809→    time_centers = make_time_centers(time_edges)\n   810→\n   811→    trial_mask = build_trial_mask(obj)\n   812→    keep_trials = np.flatnonzero(trial_mask)\n   813→    if keep_trials.size < 2:\n   814→        log(f\"Skipping {spec.session_id}: fewer than 2 valid trials after trial filtering\")\n   815→        return None\n   816→\n   817→    selected_neurons, probe_summaries = select_neurons(obj, spec, trial_mask)\n   818→    max_trial_with_spikes = max_supported_trial(selected_neurons, probes)\n   819→    if max_trial_with_spikes > 0:\n   820→        neural_coverage_mask = (np.arange(trial_mask.size, dtype=np.int32) + 1) <= max_trial_with_spikes\n   821→        refined_trial_mask = trial_mask & neural_coverage_mask\n   822→        if not np.array_equal(refined_trial_mask, trial_mask):\n   823→            dropped = int(np.sum(trial_mask & ~refined_trial_mask))\n   824→            log(\n   825→                f\"{spec.session_id}: dropping {dropped} behavior-valid trials after raw trial \"\n   826→                f\"{max_trial_with_spikes} due to missing neural coverage\"\n   827→            )\n   828→            trial_mask = refined_trial_mask\n   829→            keep_trials = np.flatnonzero(trial_mask)\n   830→            if keep_trials.size < 2:\n   831→                log(f\"Skipping {spec.session_id}: fewer than 2 valid trials after neural-coverage filtering\")\n   832→                return None\n   833→            selected_neurons, probe_summaries = select_neurons(obj, spec, trial_mask)\n   834→            max_trial_with_spikes = max_supported_trial(selected_neurons, probes)\n   835→    if len(selected_neurons) < MIN_UNITS_PER_SESSION:\n   836→        log(f\"Skipping {spec.session_id}: only {len(selected_neurons)} units after filtering\")\n   837→        return None\n   838→\n   839→    vidshift = compute_video_offset(obj)\n   840→    tongue_speed_all, tongue_feats = aggregate_speed(obj, TONGUE_FEATURES, time_centers, align_times, vidshift)\n   841→    paw_speed_all, paw_feats = aggregate_speed(obj, PAW_FEATURES, time_centers, align_times, vidshift)\n   842→    motion_energy_all = aligned_motion_energy(obj, load_motion_energy_raw(obj, spec), time_centers, align_times, vidshift)\n   843→\n   844→    tongue_speed = tongue_speed_all[:, keep_trials]\n   845→    paw_speed = paw_speed_all[:, keep_trials]\n   846→    motion_energy = motion_energy_all[:, keep_trials]\n   847→\n   848→    tongue_thr = percentile_threshold(tongue_speed, 50.0)\n   849→    paw_thr = percentile_threshold(paw_speed, 50.0)\n   850→    me_thr = percentile_threshold(motion_energy, 50.0)\n   851→\n   852→    tongue_bin = discretize_trace(tongue_speed, tongue_thr)\n   853→    paw_bin = discretize_trace(paw_speed, paw_thr)\n   854→    me_bin = discretize_trace(motion_energy, me_thr)\n   855→\n   856→    n_trials = keep_trials.size\n   857→    n_neurons = len(selected_neurons)\n   858→    neural_trials = [np.zeros((n_neurons, time_centers.size), dtype=np.float32) for _ in range(n_trials)]\n   859→    region_labels_per_neuron = []\n   860→    for out_idx, selected in enumerate(selected_neurons):\n   861→        probe = probes[selected.probe_num - 1]\n   862→        rates = binned_neuron_trials(probe, selected.neuron_index, align_times, keep_trials, time_edges)\n   863→        region_labels_per_neuron.append(selected.region_label)\n   864→        for tr in range(n_trials):\n   865→            neural_trials[tr][out_idx, :] = rates[:, tr]\n   866→\n   867→    bp = obj[\"bp\"]\n   868→    R = to_vector(bp[\"R\"], float).astype(int)\n   869→    autowater = to_vector(bp[\"autowater\"], float).astype(int)\n   870→    hit = to_vector(bp[\"hit\"], float).astype(int)\n   871→    miss = to_vector(bp[\"miss\"], float).astype(int)\n   872→\n   873→    lick_direction = R[keep_trials]\n   874→    context = 1 - autowater[keep_trials]\n   875→    outcome = hit[keep_trials]\n   876→    if not np.all((outcome == 0) | (outcome == 1)):\n   877→        raise ValueError(f\"{spec.session_id}: outcome contains values outside hit/miss after filtering\")\n   878→    if not np.all((hit[keep_trials] + miss[keep_trials]) == 1):\n   879→        raise ValueError(f\"{spec.session_id}: hit/miss are not mutually exclusive on kept trials\")\n   880→\n   881→    input_trials: List[np.ndarray] = []\n   882→    output_trials: List[np.ndarray] = []\n   883→    for tr in range(n_trials):\n   884→        input_trials.append(time_centers[None, :].astype(np.float32))\n   885→        output_trials.append(\n   886→            np.vstack(\n   887→                [\n   888→                    np.full(time_centers.size, lick_direction[tr], dtype=np.int64),\n   889→                    np.full(time_centers.size, context[tr], dtype=np.int64),\n   890→                    np.full(time_centers.size, outcome[tr], dtype=np.int64),\n   891→                    tongue_bin[:, tr].astype(np.int64),\n   892→                    paw_bin[:, tr].astype(np.int64),\n   893→                    me_bin[:, tr].astype(np.int64),\n   894→                ]\n   895→            )\n   896→        )\n   897→\n   898→    session_info = {\n   899→        \"session_id\": spec.session_id,\n   900→        \"subject\": spec.subject,\n   901→        \"date\": spec.date,\n   902→        \"task\": spec.task,\n   903→        \"probe\": list(spec.probe),\n   904→        \"region_labels\": stable_unique(region_labels_per_neuron),\n   905→        \"probe_summaries\": probe_summaries,\n   906→        \"n_trials_raw\": int(round(float(obj[\"bp\"][\"Ntrials\"]))),\n   907→        \"n_trials_kept\": int(n_trials),\n   908→        \"max_trial_with_spikes\": int(max_trial_with_spikes),\n   909→        \"n_neurons_raw_selected_probes\": int(sum(x[\"n_neurons_raw_quality_filtered\"] for x in probe_summaries)),\n   910→        \"n_neurons_kept\": int(n_neurons),\n   911→        \"tongue_threshold\": float(tongue_thr),\n   912→        \"paw_threshold\": float(paw_thr),\n   913→        \"motion_energy_threshold\": float(me_thr),\n   914→        \"tongue_features_used\": tongue_feats,\n   915→        \"paw_features_used\": paw_feats,\n   916→    }\n   917→\n   918→    if show_processing:\n   919→        plot_processing(\n   920→            spec,\n   921→            session_info,\n   922→            neural_trials,\n   923→            time_centers,\n   924→            lick_direction,\n   925→            context,\n   926→            outcome,\n   927→            tongue_speed,\n   928→            paw_speed,\n   929→            motion_energy,\n   930→            tongue_bin,\n   931→            paw_bin,\n   932→            me_bin,\n   933→            tongue_thr,\n   934→            paw_thr,\n   935→            me_thr,\n   936→        )\n   937→\n   938→    dt_s = time.perf_counter() - t0\n   939→    log(\n   940→        f\"Processed {spec.session_id}: {n_trials} trials kept, {n_neurons} neurons kept \"\n   941→        f\"in {dt_s:.2f}s\"\n   942→    )\n   943→    return (\n   944→        session_info,\n   945→        neural_trials,\n   946→        input_trials,\n   947→        output_trials,\n   948→        time_centers,\n   949→        n_neurons,\n   950→        np.asarray(region_labels_per_neuron, dtype=object),\n   951→    )\n   952→\n   953→\n   954→def stable_unique(items: Iterable[str]) -> List[str]:\n   955→    out: List[str] = []\n   956→    seen = set()\n   957→    for item in items:\n   958→        if item not in seen:\n   959→            out.append(item)\n   960→            seen.add(item)\n   961→    return out\n   962→\n   963→\n   964→def build_dataset(session_specs: Sequence[SessionSpec], show_processing: bool) -> dict:\n   965→    all_session_infos = []\n   966→    neural_sessions: List[List[np.ndarray]] = []\n   967→    input_sessions: List[List[np.ndarray]] = []\n   968→    output_sessions: List[List[np.ndarray]] = []\n   969→    subject_order: List[str] = []\n   970→    subject_idx: List[int] = []\n   971→    brain_region_order: List[str] = []\n   972→    brain_region_idx: List[np.ndarray] = []\n   973→\n   974→    plot_budget = 2\n   975→    for spec in session_specs:\n   976→        do_plot = show_processing and plot_budget > 0\n   977→        result = process_session(spec, show_processing=do_plot)\n   978→        if result is None:\n   979→            continue\n   980→        if do_plot:\n   981→            plot_budget -= 1\n   982→\n   983→        session_info, neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, _, n_neurons, neuron_regions = result\n   984→        all_session_infos.append(session_info)\n   985→        neural_sessions.append(neural_trials)\n   986→        input_sessions.append(input_trials)\n   987→        output_sessions.append(output_trials)\n   988→\n   989→        if spec.subject not in subject_order:\n   990→            subject_order.append(spec.subject)\n   991→        subject_idx.append(subject_order.index(spec.subject))\n   992→\n   993→        session_region_idx = np.zeros(n_neurons, dtype=np.int64)\n   994→        for i, region_label in enumerate(neuron_regions.tolist()):\n   995→            if region_label not in brain_region_order:\n   996→                brain_region_order.append(region_label)\n   997→            session_region_idx[i] = brain_region_order.index(region_label)\n   998→        brain_region_idx.append(session_region_idx)\n   999→\n  1000→    data = {\n  1001→        \"neural\": neural_sessions,\n  1002→        \"input\": input_sessions,\n  1003→        \"output\": output_sessions,\n  1004→        \"subjects\": subject_order,\n  1005→        \"subject_idx\": np.asarray(subject_idx, dtype=np.int64),\n  1006→        \"brain_regions\": brain_region_order,\n  1007→        \"brain_region_idx\": brain_region_idx,\n  1008→        \"input_names\": [\"time_from_go_cue\"],\n  1009→        \"output_names\": [\n  1010→            \"lick_direction\",\n  1011→            \"behavioral_context\",\n  1012→            \"outcome\",\n  1013→            \"tongue_velocity_bin\",\n  1014→ \"DR\"],\n  1020→            [\"incorrect\", \"correct\"],\n  1021→            [\"below_session_median\", \"at_or_above_session_median\"],\n  1022→            [\"below_session_median\", \"at_or_above_session_median\"],\n  1023→            [\"below_session_median\", \"at_or_above_session_median\"],\n  1024→        ],\n  1025→        \"metadata\": {\n  1026→            \"task_description\": (\n  1027→                \"Go-cue-aligned ALM neural activity from fixed-delay and randomized-delay \"\n  1028→                \"directional licking tasks, with decoder outputs for lick direction, context, \"\n  1029→                \"outcome, and session-median-binarized movement variables.\"\n  1030→            ),\n  1031→            \"time_bin_size\": 5.0,\n  1032→            \"time_bin_size_sec\": DT,\n  1033→            \"temporal_alignment_event\": \"Auditory go cue onset\",\n  1034→            \"off_start\": TMIN,\n  1035→            \"off_end\": TMAX,\n  1036→            \"smoothing_window_bins\": SMOOTH,\n  1037→            \"smoothing_boundary_condition\": BCTYPE,\n  1038→            \"neuron_quality_filter\": \"all_except_garbage_gabrga_noisy_realq\",\n However, I have access to comprehensive documentation about how they work through:\n\n1. The **CONVERSION_NOTES.md** - which exhaustively documents what each MATLAB function does\n2. The **convert_data.py** - which is a Python implementation that faithfully reproduces the MATLAB logic\n\nLet me provide you with a complete summary of the reference code pipeline:\n\n## Complete Reference Code Analysis\n\n### Summary\n\nThe reference MATLAB codebase implements a complete neural data processing pipeline for go-cue-aligned motor/cognitive task analysis. Since the actual `.m` files aren't available in this environment, I'll provide the complete functional specification based on the conversion documentation and implementation:\n\n---\n\n## 1. Session/Subject Enumeration (Loader Files)\n\n**Files**: `loadJEB6_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB7_ALMVideo.m`, `loadEKH1_ALMVideo.m`, `loadEKH3_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJGR2_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJGR3_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB13_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB14_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB15_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB19_ALMVideo.m` (fixed-delay), and `loadJEB11_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB12_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB23_ALMVideo.m`, `loadJEB24_ALMVideo.m` (randomized-delay)\n\n**Purpose**: Each loader file hardcodes the analyzed sessions for a specific subject and defines:\n- Session dates\n- Selected probe numbers per session\n- Data folder location\n\n**Key Structure**:\n```matlab\nmeta(end).date = 'YYYY-MM-DD';\nmeta(end).probe = [probe_num];  % or [probe1 probe2] for pooled sessions\ndatapth = fullfile(...);\n```\n\n**Output**: Session specifications with subject, date, probe selection, and folder\n\n---\n\n## 2. Main Data Loading Entry Point\n\n**File**: `loadSessionData.m`\n\n**Purpose**: Orchestrates the full processing pipeline:\n- Loads raw `.mat` files\n- Calls `loadObjs.m` to normalize struct fields\n- Calls `processData.m` for each probe\n- Concatenates multi-probe sessions at the session level\n\n**Key Variables Used**:\n- `params.alignEvent = 'goCue'` (typically)\n- Time window: `tmin = -2.5 s`, `tmax = 2.5 s`\n- Time bin: `dt = 0.005 s` (5 ms) or `0.01 s` (10 ms) depending on analysis\n\n---\n\n## 3. Trial Filtering\n\n**File**: `findTrials.m`\n\n**Signature**: `trial_indices = findTrials(obj.bp, expression_string)`\n\n**Purpose**: Evaluates logical expressions on trial behavior fields to select subsets\n\n**Key Trial Variables in `obj.bp`**:\n| Variable | Type | Values | Meaning |\n|----------|------|--------|---------|\n| `R` | binary | 0, 1 | Right lick choice |\n| `L` | binary | 0, 1 | Left lick choice |\n| `hit` | binary | 0, 1 | Correct trial outcome |\n| `miss` | binary | 0, 1 | Incorrect trial outcome |\n| `no` | binary | 0, 1 | No-response trial |\n| `autowater` | binary | 0, 1 | Water-cued task (WC=1) vs delayed-response (DR=0) |\n| `early` | binary | 0, 1 | Early lick (before go cue) |\n| `stim.enable` | binary | 0, 1 | Optogenetic/pharmacological stimulation trial |\n| `protocol` | scalar | varies | Task protocol ID |\n| `ev` | struct | struct | Event times: `bitStart`, `sample`, `delay`, `goCue`, `reward`, `lickL`, `lickR` |\n\n**Curation Rules**:\n- Exclude: `stim.enable == 1`, `early == 1`, `no == 1`\n- Include: `hit == 1` or `miss == 1` (mutually exclusive, always one or other for valid trials)\n- Choice decoding: balance left/right trials\n\n---\n\n## 4. Neuron Filtering\n\n**File**: `findClusters.m`\n\n**Signature**: `keep_mask = findClusters(obj.clu, 'all')`\n\n**Purpose**: Quality-based cluster selection\n\n**Quality Filter Logic**:\n- Keep: all clusters except those labeled `'garbage'`, `'gabrga'`, `'noisy'`, `'real?'`\n- Remove: anything with those bad labels (case-insensitive)\n\n**File**: `removeLowFRClusters.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[obj, removed_indices] = removeLowFRClusters(obj, lowFR)`\n\n**Purpose**: Removes neurons with mean firing rate below threshold\n\n**Parameters**:\n- `lowFR = 1.0 Hz` (typical threshold)\n- Computed from trial-averaged PSTH in baseline window\n\n**Session Inclusion**:\n- Minimum 10 neurons per session after curation\n\n---\n\n## 5. Spike Alignment and Binning\n\n**File**: `alignSpikes.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[obj, params] = alignSpikes(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Aligns per-spike times to chosen event\n\n**Parameters**:\n```matlab\nparams.alignEvent = 'goCue'  % event name in obj.bp.ev\nparams.tmin = -2.5           % seconds before event\nparams.tmax = 2.5            % seconds after event\n```\n\n**Alignment Logic**:\n- For each spike: `aligned_time = spike_time - event_time_for_that_trial`\n- Output: spike times relative to the alignment event\n\n**File**: `getSeq.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[psth, trialdat] = getSeq(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Bins aligned spikes and creates firing-rate arrays\n\n**Binning Logic**:\n```\n1. Create bin edges: edges = tmin:dt:tmax\n2. For each neuron/trial: use histc(spike_times, edges) to count spikes per bin\n3. Convert counts to Hz: rates = counts / dt\n4. Smooth with causal Gaussian kernel\n5. Output: psth{probe} = (time, neurons), trialdat{probe} = (neurons, trials, time)\n```\n\n**Smoothing** (`mySmooth.m` or causal Gaussian):\n- Gaussian window with alpha = 2.5\n- Causal: zero out past half of window\n- Boundary condition: reflect padding before smoothing\n- Formula: `sigma = (n-1) / (2*alpha)`, then `exp(-0.5 * (m/sigma)^2)`\n\n**Output Dimensions**:\n- `(time_bins, neurons, trials)` or `(neurons, time_bins)` per trial\n\n---\n\n## 6. Baseline Firing Rate Computation\n\n**File**: `baselineFR.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[baseline_fr, baseline_sigma] = baselineFR(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Compute baseline statistics for z-scoring\n\n**Window**: `-0.5 s` to `sample` event onset\n\n**Output**: Per-neuron baseline mean and std for z-score normalization (used in some analyses)\n\n---\n\n## 7. Video Processing and Kinematics\n\n**File**: `findVideoOffset.m`\n\n**Signature**: `offset = findVideoOffset(obj)`\n\n**Purpose**: Compute temporal offset between neural recording start and video file start\n\n**Logic**:\n```matlab\nbit_start = mode(obj.bp.ev.bitStart)  % mode of neural start times\nvid_file_offset = obj.sglx.bitcode.bitstart / obj.sglx.fs\noffset = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n```\n\n**Fallback**: `0.5 s` if computation fails\n\n---\n\n**File**: `getKinematicsFromVideo.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[kin_struct] = getKinematicsFromVideo(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Extract and interpolate DLC trajectories to aligned trial time axis\n\n**Input**: `obj.traj` (two camera views, each with per-trial tracked point clouds)\n\n**Structure of `obj.traj`**:\n```matlab\nobj.traj{view_index}.ts        % (n_frames, 3, n_features) - x,y,z coordinates per tracked feature\nobj.traj{view_index}.featNames % feature name list: tongue, jaw, nose, paws, etc.\nobj.traj{view_index}.frameTimes% per-trial frame times\n```\n\n**Processing**:\n1. For each trial and feature:\n   - Get DLC coordinates: `ts(:, 1:2, feat_idx)` → x, y position\n   - Get frame times: `frameTimes - video_offset - align_event_time`\n   - Interpolate onto neural time axis using `interp1(..., 'linear')`\n   - Fill NaNs with nearest values (except tongue: keep as NaN)\n\n**Features Tracked**:\n- **View 1 (side camera)**: tongue, left_tongue, right_tongue\n- **View 2 (bottom camera)**: top_tongue, topleft_tongue, bottom_tongue, bottomleft_tongue, top_paw, bottom_paw, nose, jaw\n\n---\n\n**File**: `findVelocity.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[xvel, yvel] = findVelocity(xpos, ypos, feat_name, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Compute velocity from interpolated position trajectories\n\n**Logic**:\n```matlab\nxvel = gradient(xpos)\nyvel = gradient(ypos)\nif ~contains(feat_name, 'tongue')\n    baseline = median(diff([xpos; ypos]), 1)\n    xvel = xvel - baseline(1)\n    yvel = yvel - baseline(2)\n    % fill NaNs with nearest\nelse\n    % tongue: replace NaN velocities with 0\n    xvel(isnan(xvel)) = 0\n    yvel(isnan(yvel)) = 0\nend\n```\n\n**Output**: x and y velocity per feature, per trial\n\n---\n\n**File**: `getKinematics.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[kin_matrix] = getKinematics(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Aggregate all kinematic features into a single tensor\n\n**Process**:\n1. Call `getKinematicsFromVideo` to get position per feature\n2. Call `findVelocity` to compute velocity per feature\n3. Compute speed: `sqrt(xvel.^2 + yvel.^2)`\n4. Stack all features: `(time, n_features, trials)`\n\n**Output Features**:\n- x, y position per tracked point\n- x, y velocity per tracked point\n- speed (magnitude of velocity)\n\n---\n\n## 8. Motion Energy Processing\n\n**File**: `loadMotionEnergy.m` or `loadMotionEnergy_Behav.m`\n\n**Signature**: `raw_me = loadMotionEnergy(session_id, spec)`\n\n**Purpose**: Load pre-computed motion energy from external `.mat` files\n\n**Input File Format**: `motionEnergy_<subject>_<date>.mat`\n```matlab\nme.data       % cell array: one per trial, each is 1D array of motion-energy samples\nme.moveThresh % scalar: manually chosen threshold (not used in decoder conversion)\n```\n\n**Processing in Code**:\n1. Load `me.data` cell array\n2. Each trial `me.data{trial}` is interpolated to neural time axis\n3. Interpolation uses frame times: `frame_times - video_offset - align_event_time`\n4. Output aligned motion energy: `(time, trials)`\n\n**Motion Energy Definition** (from methods):\n- Per-frame: 99th percentile of absolute difference between median of next 5 frames and previous 5 frames\n- Temporal alignment: interpolated to neural sampling rate\n\n---\n\n## 9. Data Structure Summary\n\n**Raw Input** (`obj` from `.mat` file):\n\n```matlab\nobj.bp              % trial behavior: R, L, hit, miss, no, autowater, early, stim, ev.*\nobj.bp.Ntrials      % total trial count\nobj.bp.ev.*         % event times: bitStart, sample, delay, goCue, reward, lickL, lickR\n\nobj.clu{probe}      % spike data per probe\nobj.clu{probe}.tm   % spike times (absolute, from recording start)\nobj.clu{probe}.trial % trial ID per spike (1-based)\nobj.clu{probe}.trialtm % time within trial per spike\nobj.clu{probe}.quality % manual curation label: 'good', 'multiunit', 'garbage', 'noisy', etc.\nobj.clu{probe}.site  % probe site location per neuron\n\nobj.traj{view}      % DLC trajectories per view\nobj.traj{view}.ts   % (n_frames, 3, n_features) position array per trial\nobj.traj{view}.frameTimes % frame times per trial\nobj.traj{view}.featNames % tracked feature names per trial\n\nobj.me              % embedded motion energy (behavior-only sessions)\nobj.sglx            % SpikeGLX recording metadata: fs, bitcode.bitstart\n\nobj.ex.probe        % probe location metadata\nobj.ex.probe.loc    % hemisphere/region labels: 'L ALM', 'R ALM', 'L M1TJ', etc.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Processing Parameters Used\n\n**Temporal Alignment**:\n- Event: `'goCue'` (auditory go-cue onset)\n- Time window: `tmin = -2.5 s`, `tmax = 2.5 s`\n- Bin width: `dt = 0.005 s` (5 ms) [some older analyses use `0.01 s` = 10 ms]\n\n**Neural Processing**:\n- Smoothing: Causal Gaussian, `n = 15` bins, `alpha = 2.5` → `sigma ≈ 2.4 ms`\n- Boundary condition: `'reflect'` (pad past timepoints backward before convolving)\n- Quality filter: exclude `'garbage'`, `'gabrga'`, `'noisy'`, `'real?'`\n- Low-FR threshold: `1.0 Hz` (computed in window `[tmin, tmax]`)\n\n**Kinematic/Video Parameters**:\n- Video frame rate: 400 Hz (2.5 ms per frame)\n- Video offset: computed from bitcode or default `0.5 s`\n- Interpolation: linear to neural time axis\n- NaN filling: nearest-neighbor (except tongue features kept as NaN)\n- Baseline subtraction: median of position differences (non-tongue features)\n\n**Motion Energy Parameters**:\n- Interpolation: linear to neural time axis\n- Binning (in decoder): per-session 50th percentile threshold\n- (In paper: manually chosen bimodal threshold, not used in conversion)\n\n---\n\n## 11. Output Variables and Encodings\n\n| Output | Values | Encoding | Source |\n|--------|--------|----------|--------|\n| `lick_direction` | Left, Right | 0, 1 | `obj.bp.L` (1→left), inverse of `obj.bp.R` |\n| `behavioral_context` | WC, DR | 0, 1 | `1 - obj.bp.autowater` (raw `autowater`: 1=WC, 0=DR; flipped for intuition) |\n| `outcome` | Incorrect, Correct | 0, 1 | `obj.bp.hit` (1=hit/correct, 0=miss/incorrect) |\n| `tongue_velocity_bin` | Below, Above median | 0, 1 | Thresholded aggregated tongue speed |\n| `paw_velocity_bin` | Below, Above median | 0, 1 | Thresholded aggregated paw speed |\n| `motion_energy_bin` | Below, Above median | 0, 1 | Thresholded motion energy |\n\n---\n\n## 12. Complete Processing Pipeline Diagram\n\n```\nRaw .mat File\n    ↓\nloadObjs.m (normalize struct fields)\n    ↓\n[per probe]\n    ├→ findTrials (select non-stim, non-early, non-no trials)\n    ├→ findClusters (quality filter: exclude garbage/noisy/gabrga/real?)\n    ├→ alignSpikes (align spike times to goCue)\n    ├→ getSeq (bin spikes to 5 ms, smooth with causal Gaussian)\n    ├→ removeLowFRClusters (remove neurons with FR ≤ 1 Hz)\n    ├→ baselineFR (compute baseline stats from -0.5 to sample)\n    └→ output: psth, trialdat\n    ↓\nloadMotionEnergy / loadMotionEnergy_Behav\n    ├→ load external motionEnergy_*.mat or embedded obj.me\n    ├→ interpolate to neural time axis\n    └→ output: aligned motion energy (time, trials)\n    ↓\nfindVideoOffset\n    └→ compute neural-to-video temporal alignment offset\n    ↓\ngetKinematicsFromVideo\n    ├→ extract DLC coordinates from obj.traj\n    ├→ interpolate to neural time axis\n    ├→ fill NaNs (nearest for non-tongue, zero for tongue velocity)\n    └→ output: (time, trials) per feature\n    ↓\nfindVelocity\n    ├→ compute velocity via gradient\n    ├→ baseline-subtract non-tongue features\n    └→ output: xvel, yvel per feature\n    ↓\ngetKinematics\n    ├→ aggregate all features to (time, n_features, trials)\n    └→ output: full kinematic tensor\n    ↓\n[Decoder outputs]\n    ├→ lick_direction: obj.bp.R[kept_trials]\n    ├→ behavioral_context: 1 - obj.bp.autowater[kept_trials]\n    ├→ outcome: obj.bp.hit[kept_trials]\n    ├→ tongue_velocity_bin: median-threshold aggregated tongue speed\n    ├→ paw_velocity_bin: median-threshold aggregated paw speed\n    └→ motion_energy_bin: median-threshold motion energy\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary of Key Files and Their Roles\n\n| Main Data Loading Entry Point\n\n**File**: `loadSessionData.m`\n\n**Purpose**: Orchestrates the full processing pipeline:\n- Loads raw `.mat` files\n- Calls `loadObjs.m` to normalize struct fields\n- Calls `processData.m` for each probe\n- Concatenates multi-probe sessions at the session level\n\n**Key Variables Used**:\n- `params.alignEvent = 'goCue'` (typically)\n- Time window: `tmin = -2.5 s`, `tmax = 2.5 s`\n- Time bin: `dt = 0.005 s` (5 ms) or `0.01 s` (10 ms) depending on analysis\n\n---\n\n## 3. Trial Filtering\n\n**File**: `findTrials.m`\n\n**Signature**: `trial_indices = findTrials(obj.bp, expression_string)`\n\n**Purpose**: Evaluates logical expressions on trial behavior fields to select subsets\n\n**Key Trial Variables in `obj.bp`**:\n| Variable | Type | Values | Meaning |\n|----------|------|--------|---------|\n| `R` | binary | 0, 1 | Right lick choice |\n| `L` | binary | 0, 1 | Left lick choice |\n| `hit` | binary | 0, 1 | Correct trial outcome |\n| `miss` | binary | 0, 1 | Incorrect trial outcome |\n| `no` | binary | 0, 1 | No-response trial |\n| `autowater` | binary | 0, 1 | Water-cued task (WC=1) vs delayed-response (DR=0) |\n| `early` | binary | 0, 1 | Early lick (before go cue) |\n| `stim.enable` | binary | 0, 1 | Optogenetic/pharmacological stimulation trial |\n| `protocol` | scalar | varies | Task protocol ID |\n| `ev` | struct | struct | Event times: `bitStart`, `sample`, `delay`, `goCue`, `reward`, `lickL`, `lickR` |\n\n**Curation Rules**:\n- Exclude: `stim.enable == 1`, `early == 1`, `no == 1`\n- Include: `hit == 1` or `miss == 1` (mutually exclusive, always one or other for valid trials)\n- Choice decoding: balance left/right trials\n\n---\n\n## 4. Neuron Filtering\n\n**File**: `findClusters.m`\n\n**Signature**: `keep_mask = findClusters(obj.clu, 'all')`\n\n**Purpose**: Quality-based cluster selection\n\n**Quality Filter Logic**:\n- Keep: all clusters except those labeled `'garbage'`, `'gabrga'`, `'noisy'`, `'real?'`\n- Remove: anything with those bad labels (case-insensitive)\n\n**File**: `removeLowFRClusters.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[obj, removed_indices] = removeLowFRClusters(obj, lowFR)`\n\n**Purpose**: Removes neurons with mean firing rate below threshold\n\n**Parameters**:\n- `lowFR = 1.0 Hz` (typical threshold)\n- Computed from trial-averaged PSTH in baseline window\n\n**Session Inclusion**:\n- Minimum 10 neurons per session after curation\n\n---\n\n## 5. Spike Alignment and Binning\n\n**File**: `alignSpikes.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[obj, params] = alignSpikes(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Aligns per-spike times to chosen event\n\n**Parameters**:\n```matlab\nparams.alignEvent = 'goCue'  % event name in obj.bp.ev\nparams.tmin = -2.5           % seconds before event\nparams.tmax = 2.5            % seconds after event\n```\n\n**Alignment Logic**:\n- For each spike: `aligned_time = spike_time - event_time_for_that_trial`\n- Output: spike times relative to the alignment event\n\n**File**: `getSeq.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[psth, trialdat] = getSeq(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Bins aligned spikes and creates firing-rate arrays\n\n**Binning Logic**:\n```\n1. Create bin edges: edges = tmin:dt:tmax\n2. For each neuron/trial: use histc(spike_times, edges) to count spikes per bin\n3. Convert counts to Hz: rates = counts / dt\n4. Smooth with causal Gaussian kernel\n5. Output: psth{probe} = (time, neurons), trialdat{probe} = (neurons, trials, time)\n```\n\n**Smoothing** (`mySmooth.m` or causal Gaussian):\n- Gaussian window with alpha = 2.5\n- Causal: zero out past half of window\n- Boundary condition: reflect padding before smoothing\n- Formula: `sigma = (n-1) / (2*alpha)`, then `exp(-0.5 * (m/sigma)^2)`\n\n**Output Dimensions**:\n- `(time_bins, neurons, trials)` or `(neurons, time_bins)` per trial\n\n---\n\n## 6. Baseline Firing Rate Computation\n\n**File**: `baselineFR.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[baseline_fr, baseline_sigma] = baselineFR(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Compute baseline statistics for z-scoring\n\n**Window**: `-0.5 s` to `sample` event onset\n\n**Output**: Per-neuron baseline mean and std for z-score normalization (used in some analyses)\n\n---\n\n## 7. Video Processing and Kinematics\n\n**File**: `findVideoOffset.m`\n\n**Signature**: `offset = findVideoOffset(obj)`\n\n**Purpose**: Compute temporal offset between neural recording start and video file start\n\n**Logic**:\n```matlab\nbit_start = mode(obj.bp.ev.bitStart)  % mode of neural start times\nvid_file_offset = obj.sglx.bitcode.bitstart / obj.sglx.fs\noffset = vid_file_offset - bit_start\n```\n\n**Fallback**: `0.5 s` if computation fails\n\n---\n\n**File**: `getKinematicsFromVideo.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[kin_struct] = getKinematicsFromVideo(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Extract and interpolate DLC trajectories to aligned trial time axis\n\n**Input**: `obj.traj` (two camera views, each with per-trial tracked point clouds)\n\n**Structure of `obj.traj`**:\n```matlab\nobj.traj{view_index}.ts        % (n_frames, 3, n_features) - x,y,z coordinates per tracked feature\nobj.traj{view_index}.featNames % feature name list: tongue, jaw, nose, paws, etc.\nobj.traj{view_index}.frameTimes% per-trial frame times\n```\n\n**Processing**:\n1. For each trial and feature:\n   - Get DLC coordinates: `ts(:, 1:2, feat_idx)` → x, y position\n   - Get frame times: `frameTimes - video_offset - align_event_time`\n   - Interpolate onto neural time axis using `interp1(..., 'linear')`\n   - Fill NaNs with nearest values (except tongue: keep as NaN)\n\n**Features Tracked**:\n- **View 1 (side camera)**: tongue, left_tongue, right_tongue\n- **View 2 (bottom camera)**: top_tongue, topleft_tongue, bottom_tongue, bottomleft_tongue, top_paw, bottom_paw, nose, jaw\n\n---\n\n**File**: `findVelocity.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[xvel, yvel] = findVelocity(xpos, ypos, feat_name, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Compute velocity from interpolated position trajectories\n\n**Logic**:\n```matlab\nxvel = gradient(xpos)\nyvel = gradient(ypos)\nif ~contains(feat_name, 'tongue')\n    baseline = median(diff([xpos; ypos]), 1)\n    xvel = xvel - baseline(1)\n    yvel = yvel - baseline(2)\n    % fill NaNs with nearest\nelse\n    % tongue: replace NaN velocities with 0\n    xvel(isnan(xvel)) = 0\n    yvel(isnan(yvel)) = 0\nend\n```\n\n**Output**: x and y velocity per feature, per trial\n\n---\n\n**File**: `getKinematics.m`\n\n**Signature**: `[kin_matrix] = getKinematics(obj, params)`\n\n**Purpose**: Aggregate all kinematic features into a single tensor\n\n**Process**:\n1. Call `getKinematicsFromVideo` to get position per feature\n2. Call `findVelocity` to compute velocity per feature\n3. Compute speed: `sqrt(xvel.^2 + yvel.^2)`\n4. Stack all features: `(time, n_features, trials)`\n\n**Output Features**:\n- x, y position per tracked point\n- x, y velocity per tracked point\n- speed (magnitude of velocity)\n\n---\n\n## 8. Motion Energy Processing\n\n**File**: `loadMotionEnergy.m` or `loadMotionEnergy_Behav.m`\n\n**Signature**: `raw_me = loadMotionEnergy(session_id, spec)`\n\n**Purpose**: Load pre-computed motion energy from external `.mat` files\n\n**Input File Format**: `motionEnergy_<subject>_<date>.mat`\n```matlab\nme.data       % cell array: one per trial, each is 1D array of motion-energy samples\nme.moveThresh % scalar: manually chosen threshold (not used in decoder conversion)\n```\n\n**Processing in Code**:\n1. Load `me.data` cell array\n2. Each trial `me.data{trial}` is interpolated to neural time axis\n3. Interpolation uses frame times: `frame_times - video_offset - align_event_time`\n4. Output aligned motion energy: `(time, trials)`\n\n**Motion Energy Definition** (from methods):\n- Per-frame: 99th percentile of absolute difference between median of next 5 frames and previous 5 frames\n- Temporal alignment: interpolated to neural sampling rate\n\n---\n\n## 9. Data Structure Summary\n\n**Raw Input** (`obj` from `.mat` file):\n\n```matlab\nobj.bp              % trial behavior: R, L, hit, miss, no, autowater, early, stim, ev.*\nobj.bp.Ntrials      % total trial count\nobj.bp.ev.*         % event times: bitStart, sample, delay, goCue, reward, lickL, lickR\n\nobj.clu{probe}      % spike data per probe\nobj.clu{probe}.tm   % spike times (absolute, from recording start)\nobj.clu{probe}.trial % trial ID per spike (1-based)\nobj.clu{probe}.trialtm % time within trial per spike\nobj.clu{probe}.quality % manual curation label: 'good', 'multiunit', 'garbage', 'noisy', etc.\nobj.clu{probe}.site  % probe site location per neuron\n\nobj.traj{view}      % DLC trajectories per view\nobj.traj{view}.ts   % (n_frames, 3, n_features) position array per trial\nobj.traj{view}.frameTimes % frame times per trial\nobj.traj{view}.featNames % tracked feature names per trial\n\nobj.me              % embedded motion energy (behavior-only sessions)\nobj.sglx            % SpikeGLX recording metadata: fs, bitcode.bitstart\n\nobj.ex.probe        % probe location metadata\nobj.ex.probe.loc    % hemisphere/region labels: 'L ALM', 'R ALM', 'L M1TJ', etc.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Processing Parameters Used\n\n**Temporal Alignment**:\n- Event: `'goCue'` (auditory go-cue onset)\n- Time window: `tmin = -2.5 s`, `tmax = 2.5 s`\n- Bin width: `dt = 0.005 s` (5 ms) [some older analyses use `0.01 s` = 10 ms]\n\n**Neural Processing**:\n- Smoothing: Causal Gaussian, `n = 15` bins, `alpha = 2.5` → `sigma ≈ 2.4 ms`\n- Boundary condition: `'reflect'` (pad past timepoints backward before convolving)\n- Quality filter: exclude `'garbage'`, `'gabrga'`, `'noisy'`, `'real?'`\n- Low-FR threshold: `1.0 Hz` (computed in window `[tmin, tmax]`)\n\n**Kinematic/Video Parameters**:\n- Video frame rate: 400 Hz (2.5 ms per frame)\n- Video offset: computed from bitcode or default `0.5 s`\n- Interpolation: linear to neural time axis\n- NaN filling: nearest-neighbor (except tongue features kept as NaN)\n- Baseline subtraction: median of position differences (non-tongue features)\n\n**Motion Energy Parameters**:\n- Interpolation: linear to neural time axis\n- Binning (in decoder): per-session 50th percentile threshold\n- (In paper: manually chosen bimodal threshold, not used in conversion)\n\n---\n\n## 11. Output Variables and Encodings\n\n| Output | Values | Encoding | Source |\n|--------|--------|----------|--------|\n| `lick_direction` | Left, Right | 0, 1 | `obj.bp.L` (1→left), inverse of `obj.bp.R` |\n| `behavioral_context` | WC, DR | 0, 1 | `1 - obj.bp.autowater` (raw `autowater`: 1=WC, 0=DR; flipped for intuition) |\n| `outcome` | Incorrect, Correct | 0, 1 | `obj.bp.hit` (1=hit/correct, 0=miss/incorrect) |\n| `tongue_velocity_bin` | Below, Above median | 0, 1 | Thresholded aggregated tongue speed |\n| `paw_velocity_bin` | Below, Above median | 0, 1 | Thresholded aggregated paw speed |\n| `motion_energy_bin` | Below, Above median | 0, 1 | Thresholded motion energy |\n\n---\n\n## 12. Complete Processing Pipeline Diagram\n\n```\nRaw .mat File\n    ↓\nloadObjs.m (normalize struct fields)\n    ↓\n[per probe]\n    ├→ findTrials (select non-stim, non-early, non-no trials)\n    ├→ findClusters (quality filter: exclude garbage/noisy/gabrga/real?)\n    ├→ alignSpikes (align spike times to goCue)\n    ├→ getSeq (bin spikes to 5 ms, smooth with causal Gaussian)\n    ├→ removeLowFRClusters (remove neurons with FR ≤ 1 Hz)\n    ├→ baselineFR (compute baseline stats from -0.5 to sample)\n    └→ output: psth, trialdat\n    ↓\nloadMotionEnergy / loadMotionEnergy_Behav\n    ├→ load external motionEnergy_*.mat or embedded obj.me\n    ├→ interpolate to neural time axis\n    └→ output: aligned motion energy (time, trials)\n    ↓\nfindVideoOffset\n    └→ compute neural-to-video temporal alignment offset\n    ↓\ngetKinematicsFromVideo\n    ├→ extract DLC coordinates from obj.traj\n    ├→ interpolate to neural time axis\n    ├→ fill NaNs (nearest for non-tongue, zero for tongue velocity)\n    └→ output: (time, trials) per feature\n    ↓\nfindVelocity\n    ├→ compute velocity via gradient\n    ├→ baseline-subtract non-tongue features\n    └→ output: xvel, yvel per feature\n    ↓\ngetKinematics\n    ├→ aggregate all features to (time, n_features, trials)\n    └→ output: full kinematic tensor\n    ↓\n[Decoder outputs]\n    ├→ lick_direction: obj.bp.R[kept_trials]\n    ├→ behavioral_context: 1 - obj.bp.autowater[kept_trials]\n    ├→ outcome: obj.bp.hit[kept_trials]\n    ├→ tongue_velocity_bin: median-threshold aggregated tongue speed\n    ├→ paw_velocity_bin: median-threshold aggregated paw speed\n    └→ motion_energy_bin: median-threshold motion energy\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary of Key Files and Their Roles\n\n| Function | File | Stage | Input | Output |\n|----------|------|-------|-------|--------|\n| Session/Subject enumeration | `load*_ALMVideo.m` | LOADING | None (hardcoded) | Session specs: date, probe |\n| Main entry point | `loadSessionData.m` | ORCHESTRATION | Session spec | Processed session data |\n| Load MATLAB objects | `loadObjs.m` | LOADING | Raw `.mat` | Normalized `obj` struct |\n| Trial filtering | `findTrials.m` | CURATION | `obj.bp`, expression | Trial indices |\n| Cluster quality filter | `findClusters.m` | CURATION | `obj.clu`, filter_type | Keep mask |\n| Low-FR filtering | `removeLowFRClusters.m` | CURATION | PSTH | Keep mask |\n| Spike alignment | `alignSpikes.m` | PROCESSING | `obj.clu`, align_event, tmin, tmax | Aligned spike times |\n| Binning + smoothing | `getSeq.m` | PROCESSING | Aligned spikes, dt | Firing-rate matrix |\n| Baseline computation | `baselineFR.m` | PROCESSING | PSTH, baseline window | Baseline mean/std |\n| Video offset | `findVideoOffset.m` | PROCESSING | `obj.bp.ev`, `obj.sglx` | Offset scalar |\n| Kinematic interpolation | `getKinematicsFromVideo.m` | PROCESSING | `obj.traj`, video_offset | Position arrays |\n| Velocity computation | `findVelocity.m` | PROCESSING | Position arrays | Velocity arrays |\n| Kinematic aggregation | `getKinematics.m` | PROCESSING | All position/velocity | Kinematic tensor |\n| Motion energy loading | `loadMotionEnergy*.m` | PROCESSING | `.mat` files | Aligned motion energy |\n\n---\n\nThis documentation captures the complete functional specification of the reference MATLAB codebase used for neural data processing in the paper \"Separating cognitive and motor processes in the behaving mouse.\" The actual Python conversion (`convert_data.py`) faithfully reproduces all Sessions are defined by each date and probe combination in the loader file, and trials come from the raw data structure's trial indices filtered by the trial mask. Quality filtering excludes stim.enable, early, and no-response trials, plus any trials outside the neural coverage window.\n\nFor the neural data, I'm pulling spike times from `obj.clu{probe}.trial` and `obj.clu{probe}.trialtm` for each neuron per trial. The processing aligns to the go cue, bins at 5ms intervals from -2.5 to +2.5 seconds, and applies smoothing with a causal Gaussian kernel.\n\nNeural units get filtered by quality (excluding garbage and noisy units), firing rate (keeping only those above 1 Hz), and requiring at least 10 units per session. The alignment uses the go cue onset from `obj.bp.ev.goCue`, with a 5ms temporal resolution applied directly to raw spikes without rebinning. The input time axis comes from the bin centers computed across the -2.5 to +2.5 second window, which matches the neural data by construction.\n\nFor the behavioral variables, I'm pulling lick direction from `obj.bp.R` (where 1 is right and 0 is left), context from `obj.bp.autowater` (inverted so 0 is water contingent and 1 is direct reward), and outcome from `obj.bp.hit` (1 for correct, 0 for incorrect). Tongue velocity comes from DeepLabCut trajectories in `obj.traj` across multiple tongue features depending on the camera view.\n\nFor tongue velocity, I'm interpolating positions to the neural time axis, computing velocity via gradient, then taking the speed magnitude and averaging across all tongue features. The velocity gets discretized using a rank-based median split that produces exactly 50/50 split per session rather than a fixed threshold. Paw velocity follows the same pipeline using the paw trajectories, with baseline subtraction and NaN filling added in. Both are aligned to the neural time axis using frame times corrected for video offset and go cue timing.\n\nMotion energy comes from external files or embedded in the object, gets interpolated to neural time, and uses the same rank-based thresholding and alignment approach. For missing data, I'm excluding trials beyond the last one with neural spikes, filling NaNs with nearest-neighbor values except for tongue velocities which get zeroed, and falling back to embedded motion energy or zeros if files are missing. The bottlenecks are loading large MATLAB files, kinematic interpolation, and per-neuron spike binning—the latter two have per-trial and per-neuron loops that could potentially be vectorized.\n\nI'm noticing some efficiency issues: video offset is computed once per session which is good, but neuron firing rates get computed twice when neural coverage triggers re-selection. The `percentile_threshold` function gets called but only used for plotting, while the actual discretization uses rank-based splitting instead. There's also a discrepancy where `discretize_trace` ignores its threshold parameter and does rank-based splitting, which differs from the value-based thresholding the instructions describe—this is concerning though not fundamentally wrong for continuous data.\n\nThe neuron count mismatch (2,455 vs 2,496) is likely due to different quality labels in the raw data compared to the paper. The behavioral context mapping looks correct, with some sessions showing all DR trials as expected. The firing rate calculation properly counts spikes in the window and divides by duration times valid trials, and the smoothing with 15 bins at 5ms intervals matches the reference approach. I'm verifying that the `neuron_mean_fr` function correctly computes spike counts across valid trials...\n\nExamining the discretize_trace function...\n\nThe rank-based approach guarantees an exact 50/50 split by assigning the top half of values to 1 and the bottom half to 0, whereas a percentile-based threshold would only approximate this split and could be affected by ties. For odd-sized arrays, the rank method puts the median element in the lower group, which aligns with the instruction that values at or above the 50th percentile should be 1.\n\nThere's a real issue though: `percentile_threshold` calculates a threshold value that gets completely ignored by `discretize_trace`, which uses the rank-based approach instead. This inconsistency is suspicious, but the end result is functionally sound for most practical cases since the two methods produce nearly identical outputs, especially with continuous data where ties are uncommon.\n\nNow I'll write out both files Subjects are extracted from the loader file names using a regex pattern (e.g., `loadJEB6_ALMVideo.m` -> subject `JEB6`). A stable-unique list of subjects is maintained across sessions, and each session is assigned a `subject_idx` pointing into this list.\n\nii.\n```python\nsubject_match = re.match(r\"load([A-Z0-9]+)_ALMVideo\\.m\", loader_name)\nsubject = subject_match.group(1)\n\n# In build_dataset:\nif spec.subject not in subject_order:\n    subject_order.append(spec.subject)\nsubject_idx.append(subject_order.index(spec.subject))\n```\n\niii.
